# U2 — Layer 1 conformance: every proposal edit read against Layer 1, graded, with its disposition after wave 3

## Status: WORK LEDGER (unification lane U2 of `UNIFICATION_BRIEF.md`, test 7, written by repair lane R-B of `unified/U6_VERDICT_AND_REPAIRS.md` finding 3; reference, resolves nothing at runtime, CLAUDE.md §0.05; a spec that binds code is never edited to match the code, so nothing here edits Layer 1; deleted when workplan v12 is adopted)

A-98 vocabulary lane (2026-11-12, A-98; ledger `unified/work/A98_SWEEP.md`): "capability" in prose → skill (17); "trained score/rank" → skill score (1); "training" for the rank → skill score (1); proposed names `talent_level` → `attribute_level`, `pool.py::talent` → `pool.py::governing_attribute` and test names (2); rewordings by sense (1: retired kinds `aptitude`/`trait`, traits apart, Thread Sensitivity not a skill, line-wrapped "trained").

## Read at: the working tree of branch `ccr-62958c70-0kjhsw`, 2026-11-12, after waves 1 to 3 and the U6 verdict. Extracted by a Bash script (scratch, not committed) from section 1 of `unified/work/R1_READ.md` to `R5_READ.md`, which this file replaces as the record before `unified/work/` is deleted.

## 0. What this table is, who graded it, and what was and was not checked

**Source and method.** Section 1 of each read lane's output (the lane partition is `unified/UNIFICATION_RUN.md`: R1 (PC, MB, DICE, GO); R2 (CORE, CHAR, SC, FI, ARCS, LACUNA, scene access); R3 (FA, SE, WR, stances, story enablement); R4 (verbs, institutions); R5 (cross-cutting: 16, 17, 18, 20 to 23)) is a set of markdown tables with six columns: proposal file, edit, Layer 1 row, grade, verdict, rewrite or conflict. A script split every row (cells containing `|` inside backticks are kept whole), dropped the header and separator rows and the summary and finding tables, and kept **552 rows**: R1 123, R2 91, R3 62, R4 175, R5 101. Twenty lines were not made rows because they are not conformance rows (the R1 summary table, 4 lines, and the R4 `F-R4-nn` finding table, 16 lines); five R3 rows with an unquoted `|` inside a quotation were recovered by taking the last three cells as grade, verdict and rewrite and joining the middle cells with ` | ` into the Layer 1 cell. The originals stay in `work/R*_READ.md` until they are deleted. Cell text is the read lane's, unedited.

**Who graded.** The grades and verdicts were produced by **the five read lanes** (R1 to R5), each reading its family against `architecture/meta/04_CODE_ARCHITECTURE.md` (and `holonic_ARCHITECTURE.md` §43 and §45 to §47 for modules) by the `skills/layer-conformance/SKILL.md` Lens B method: a "grade" is `04` §0's grade of the **Layer 1 row tested** (what the row guarantees: STRUCTURAL, MECHANICAL, CONVENTION), not of the proposal; a "verdict" is CONFORMS, VIOLATES or AMBIGUOUS of the proposal's edit against that row.

**What was independently checked.** The Fable verdict (`unified/U6_VERDICT_AND_REPAIRS.md`, "Not checked by the verdict") records that it spot-checked **ten edits** and did not check "the read-lane grades beyond ten edits". That file does not list which ten, and a search of the suite for "ten edits" and "spot-check" finds nothing else that does (`unified/U6_FOR_JORDAN.md` spot-checks rulings, not these rows), so this table **cannot say which rows were the ten**; treat every row as graded once, by the read lane that wrote it, and none as independently confirmed. In particular the CONFORMS rows, which no later lane re-read, rest on the read lane's reading alone, and the wave-3 rewrite lanes acted only on VIOLATES and AMBIGUOUS rows (`work/R1_READ.md` §1.5).

**The last column** is the row's disposition after wave 3, by script from three sources and **not re-verified by reading each proposal**. A CONFORMS row reads "conforms". A VIOLATES or AMBIGUOUS row reads: "rewritten" when a `work/W3_*_DONE.md` table lists the row's proposal file and its "applied" cell cites section 1 of the read lane's output (whether this particular row was among those applied is not checked); "file rewritten in wave 3 … does not cite section 1" when a DONE table lists the file but cites other sections only (the rewrite may or may not have applied this row); and "amendment owed" when the row's text names a ruling for which `unified/U3_LAYER1_AMENDMENTS.md` has a node (cited by node id), or says "owed" (then `unified/OWED_TO_UNIFICATION.md` §A, no node matched by ruling number). Both can apply to one row: the code is rewritten to the form the dormant control arm permits and the arm that contradicts the sentence waits on the amendment. A row the script could not tie to any of these reads "no wave-3 record found by script; open the proposal"; those are **unresolved by this extraction, not conforming**.

**Counts of the extracted rows.** By verdict word: CONFORMS 352, AMBIGUOUS 86, VIOLATES 77, other 37. By disposition (first clause of the last column): conforms 364, file rewritten in wave 3 90, rewritten 50, no wave-3 record found for this row by script 23, superseded or withdrawn row 16, amendment owed 9.

A conflict between Jordan's stated intent and a ratified Layer 1 rule is never resolved by editing Layer 1 (`UNIFICATION_BRIEF.md` test 7); the amendments the rulings owe are `unified/U3_LAYER1_AMENDMENTS.md` (both halves of each rule, the batch each gates and the arm allowed before it).

## 2. Closing note: the Layer 1 amendment of 2026-11-12 (Jordan, A-97; `ED-IN-0358`)

Every row above whose disposition reads "amendment owed: `L1-…`" was graded against a Layer 1 sentence that node has
now amended, both halves (`unified/U3_LAYER1_AMENDMENTS.md`, `unified/work/L1_APPLIED.md`). The "amendment owed"
clause of these rows is discharged, and each is read as **conforming after its node** (A-97 being Jordan's act, not
Layer 1 edited to match code). This note does not re-read the proposals: a row conforms only as far as its edit says
what the amended sentence now permits.
